class Solution { public int findNumberOfMinimumValue ( int root ) { if ( root == null ) return - 1 ; int ans = Integer . MAX_VALUE ; int min_val = root ; Stack < Integer > stack = new Stack < > ( ) ; stack . add ( root ) ; while ( ! stack . isEmpty ( ) ) { int curr = stack . peek ( ) ; if ( curr == null ) continue ; if ( min_val < ans ) { stack . add ( curr . left ) ; } else if ( min_val < ans ) { stack . add ( curr . left ) ; stack . add ( curr . right ) ; } } return ans ; } }
class Solution { public int removeNthFromEnd ( int head , int n ) { if ( head == null ) return null ; int slow = head ; for ( int i = 0 ; i < n ; i ++ ) { fast = fast . next ; } if ( fast == null ) { fast = fast . next ; return head ; } while ( fast != null ) { fast = fast . next ; slow = slow . next ; return head ; } } }
